		#pop scope
		pop = { #for a specific pop
			add = {
				trigger = { #for females to be added, the species must not be all male/futa. If the species is all female, females will be added regardless of vf policy.
					exists = species
					NOR = { 
						has_trait = trait_lv_futanari
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_male
						species = { species_gender = male }
						species = { species_gender = indeterminable }
					}
					OR = {
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
						species = { species_gender = female }
						OR = {
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_default }
						}
					}
				}
				portraits = {
					06MamF1 06MamF2 06MamF3 06MamF4 06MamF5 06MamF6 06MamF7 06MamF8

						species = { species_gender = male }
						OR = {
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_default }
						}
					}
				}
				portraits = {
					06MamM1 06MamM2 06MamM3 06MamM4 06MamM5 06MamM6 06MamM7 06MamM8 
					06MamM9 06MamM10 06MamM11 06MamM12 06MamM13 06MamM14 06MamM15 
					06MamM16 06MamM17 06MamM18 06MamM19 06MamM20 

				}
			}
			add = {
				trigger = {
					exists = species
					OR = { #futas will be added if the species has futanari trait, indeterminable gender, or if it is not all male/female and uses a vf policy. 
						has_trait = trait_lv_futanari
						species = { species_gender = indeterminable }
						AND = {
							NOR = { 
								has_trait = trait_lv_all_male
								has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
								species = { species_gender = female }
								species = { species_gender = male }
							}
							OR = {
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				portraits = {
					06MamH1 06MamH2 06MamH3 06MamH4 06MamH5 06MamH6 06MamH7 06MamH8 
					06MamH9 06MamH10 06MamH11 06MamH12 06MamH13 06MamH14 06MamH15 
					06MamH16 06MamH17 06MamH18 06MamH19 06MamH20 06MamH21 

				}
			}
		}
		#leader scope
		#default: males and females as normal. malefuta: females replaced with futas. femalefuta: males replaced with futas. all: futas included as females. 
		leader = {
			add = { #only females will be added to the female gender for default or femalefuta policies, or if the species is all female. 
				trigger = { 
					OR = {
						NOR = { 
							species = { has_trait = trait_lv_futanari }
							gender = indeterminable
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
						}
						species = { has_trait = trait_lv_all_female }
					}
					gender = female
				}
				portraits = {
					06MamF1 06MamF2 06MamF3 06MamF4 06MamF5 06MamF6 06MamF7 06MamF8 
					06MamF9 06MamF10 06MamF11 06MamF12 06MamF13 06MamF14 06MamF15 
					06MamF16 06MamF17 06MamF18 06MamF19 
	
				}
			}
			add = { #females and futas will be added to the female gender the all policy and is not all female.
				trigger = { 
					NOR = { 
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
						species = { species_gender = female }
						species = { has_trait = trait_lv_futanari }
						gender = indeterminable
						owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
						owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }

				}
			}

					OR = {
						NOR = {

					06MamM16 06MamM17 06MamM18 06MamM19 06MamM20 

				}
			}

					NOR = { 
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_male
						species = { species_gender = male }

					}

				portraits = {

						has_trait = trait_lv_futanari
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
						species = { species_gender = female }
						species = { species_gender = indeterminable }
					}
					OR = {
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_male
						species = { species_gender = male }
						OR = {
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_default }
						}
					}
				}
				portraits = {
					07MamM1 07MamM2 07MamM3 07MamM4 07MamM5 07MamM6 07MamM7 07MamM8 
					07MamM9 07MamM10 07MamM11 07MamM12 07MamM13 07MamM14 07MamM15 
					07MamM16 07MamM17 07MamM18 07MamM19 07MamM20 07MamM21 07MamM22 
					07MamM23 

				}
			}
			add = {
				trigger = {
					exists = species
					OR = { #futas will be added if the species has futanari trait, indeterminable gender, or if it is not all male/female and uses a vf policy. 
						has_trait = trait_lv_futanari
						species = { species_gender = indeterminable }
						AND = {
							NOR = { 
								has_trait = trait_lv_all_male
								has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
								species = { species_gender = female }
								species = { species_gender = male }
							}
							OR = {
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }
								owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
							}
						}
					}
				}
				portraits = {
					07MamH1 07MamH2 07MamH3 07MamH4 07MamH5 07MamH6 07MamH7 07MamH8 
					07MamH9 07MamH10 07MamH11 07MamH12 07MamH13 07MamH14 07MamH15 
					07MamH16 07MamH17 

				}
			}
		}
		#leader scope
		#default: males and females as normal. malefuta: females replaced with futas. femalefuta: males replaced with futas. all: futas included as females. 
		leader = {
			add = { #only females will be added to the female gender for default or femalefuta policies, or if the species is all female. 
				trigger = { 
					OR = {
						NOR = { 
							species = { has_trait = trait_lv_futanari }
							gender = indeterminable
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
							owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
						}
						species = { has_trait = trait_lv_all_female }
					}
					gender = female
				}
				portraits = {
					07MamF1 07MamF2 07MamF3 07MamF4 07MamF5 07MamF6 07MamF7 07MamF8 
					07MamF9 07MamF10 07MamF11 07MamF12 07MamF13 07MamF14 07MamF15 
					07MamF16 07MamF17 07MamF18 
	
				}
			}
			add = { #females and futas will be added to the female gender the all policy and is not all female.
				trigger = { 
					NOR = { 
						has_trait = trait_lv_all_female
						species = { species_gender = female }
						species = { has_trait = trait_lv_futanari }
						gender = indeterminable
						owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_malefuta }
						owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }

						has_trait = trait_lv_all_male

			add = { #only futas will be added to the male gender for femalefuta policy, if the species is not all male.

						owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_all }
						}
					owner = { has_policy_flag = vf_gender_femalefuta }
					gender = male

					09MamH16 09MamH17 09MamH18 09MamH19 

				}
			}

						}
